part III^ec. 4] THE GAZETTE OF INDIA, JANUARY 24, 1976 (MAGHA 4, 1897) 


RESERVE BANK OF INDJA 
CENTRAL office' ' 

Department of Accounts and Expenditure 
Bombay, the 24th January, 1976 

In pursuance of Rule 18 of the Rules made by the Government of India under Section 28 of the Public Debt Act, 1944 and pub- 
lished in the Gazette of India of the 20th April 1946 (as amended under Notification No, F,(S)(70)-B/52 dated the 29th April 1954) the 
following list (for the quarter ended 30th June, 1974) is hereby advertised of securities lost etc. in respect of which prima facie grounds 
exist for believing that the securities have been lost and that the claim of applicants is just. All persons other than the respective claimants 
named below who have any claim upon these securities should communicate immediately with the Chief Accountant, Reserve Bank of 
India, Central Office, Department of Accounts and Expenditure, Central Debt Section, Bombay. 

The list has been divided into two parts — Part ‘A‘ being securities now advertised for the first time and Part ‘B’ the list of securities 
previously advertisetl. i

STATE BANK OP INDIA 
CENTRAL OFFICE 
Bombay, the 16th December 1975 
NOTICES 

The following appointments on the Bank’s staff are hcrbv 
notified ; — 

Sarvashri I. S.Varshneya and N. N. Mahajan have assum- 
ed charge as General Manager and Dy. General Manager, 
International Division. Central Office, respectively, as from 
the 27tli lune 1975. 

The Executive Committee of the Central Board has decided 
that the incumbents of the posts of the General Manager, 
International Division and the Dy. General Manager, Interna- 
tional Division, at Central Office, Bombay, be authorised to 
exercise the signing powers specified in Regulation 76 (1) of 
the State Bank of India General Regulations, 

By order of the Executive Committee of the Central Board. 

P.C.D. NAMBIAR, Dy. M.D. 

(Personnel & Services) 


STATE BANK OF INDORE 
HEAD OFFICE 
Indore, the 2nd January 1976 
NOTICE 

With reference to the Notice of the 12th November, 1975 
issued in terms of Regulation 30(2) of the Subsidiary Banks 
General Regulations, 1959 regrading the holding of a General 
Meeting of the Shareholders of the State Bank of Indore at 
the Head Office of the Bank for the purpose of electing two 
persons to be Directors of the Board of the ‘Bank in pursuance, 
of Section 25(1) (d) of the State Bank of India (Subsidiary 
Banks) Act, 1959, to fill the vacancies which will arise on the 
15th lanuary, 1976 through the retirement of (1) Shri &tish 
Chandra Malhotra, ’Windmere’, Cuffe Parade, Colaba, 
Bombay-5 and (2) Shri Lakshman Prasad Bhargava, Babu 
Ramprasad Bhargava Marg, Ujjain NOTICE IS HEREBY 
GIVEN that 1 have accepted as valid the nominations pro- 
posing the names of (1) Siri Satish Chandra Malthorta, 
‘Windmere’ Cuffe Parade, Colaba, Bombay-5 and (2) Shri 
Lakshman Prasad Bhargava, Babu Ramprasad Bhargava 
Marg, Ujjhin as candidates for election as Directors of the 
Board of the State Bank of Indore. The said nominations 
being the only valid nominations received, (1) Shri SatLsh 
Chandra Malhotra, ‘Windmere*, Cuffe Parade, Colaba, Bom- 
bay-5, and (2) Shri Lakshman Prasad Bhargava, Babu Ram 
Prasad Bhargava Marg, Ujjain shall be deemed to be elected as 
Directors of the Board of the Bank at the said General Meeting 
of the Shareholders proposed to be held on the 16th January, 
1976 which meeting in terms of Regulation 33(1) of the said 
Regulations now stands cancelled. 

B. K. MOOKERJEA, Managing Director.

935 


Part hi— Sec. 4] THE GAZHTTH OF mDIA, JANUARY 24, 1976 <MAGHA 4, 1897) 


Explanatory note on the above amendments : 

The following is the Explanatory Note on the amendments 

roposcd in Notification No. CWR(3)/75 dated 15th Deccm- 

cr 1975, This note is intended only to clarify the intention 
of the Council underlying these amendments and should not 
be construed as limiting or amplifying the scope of these 
amendments in any manner whatsoever. 

Item r. In the COst and Works Accountants Act and in the 
Cost and Works Accountants Regulations the term “Constitu- 
encies" has been used. For sake of uniformity the word 
“Regions" is being changed to “Constituencies”, in Regula- 
tion 54. , j 

Items 11 & 111. Once the election programme is decided 
upon by the Council, the publication of Voters' list and of the 
Notice calling for nominations of candidates for election is 
the responsibility of the Secretary, 

The present requirement of the Regulations is that notice 
calling for nominations etc. is to he published in the lournal of 
the Institute. Since the date of issuing notice and the date of 
publication of the Journal may not coincide, it has been 
decided to publish the notice in the Journal as routine but not 
as statutory obligation. The list of Voters and a copy of the 
election notice shall be sent to each member of the Institute 
as a statutory obligation. 

Item V. According to existing Regulation 60(1) an appeal 
against the Secretary’s refusal or rejection or acc^tanco of 
a nomination may be preferred to the Council. Since the 
Council cannot meet within the short time available under 
the Flection Regulations to refer such appeal to the Appeal 
Committee, it has been decided that the Secretary should have 
the authority to refer the appeal to the Appeal Committee 
without waiting for the Council to take action. Hence the 
cuggested amendment. 

I(em VI. According to existing Regulation 63E a candidate 
is entitled to appoint any number of persons as his authorised 
representatives to be present on his behalf at the polling 
booths. The proposed amendment seeks to restrict the 
appointment of not more than two persons for each polling 
booth and such persons shall be members of the institute. 

Item VIl, The printed Voters' Lists are so narrowly spaced 
that they cannot be conveniently used for obtaining signatures 
of the voters. It is proposed that separate forms will be used 
for taking the signatures of the voters. Also, verification with 
the specimen signature is being dispensed with as many voters 
do not send their specimen signatures. 

Item VIII. Since Section 10(2) of the Act provides for 
dealing with election disputes, the proviso to Regulation 69A 
is redundant and hence is being deleted. 

S, N, GHOSE, Secy.

UNIT TRUST OF INDIA 
New Delhi, the 31st December 1975 

No. UT. 7437/NP48-75/76.— The Unit Scheme, 1976 
made by the Board of Trustees of the Unit Trust of India 
under Section 21 of the Unit Trust of India Act, 1963, at its 
meeting held on 2nd December 1975 is published herewith for 
general information : 

UNIT SCHEME, 1976 

In exercise ol the powers conferred by Section 21 of the 
Unit Trust of India Act, 1963 (52 of 1963), the Board of 
the Unit Trust of India hereby makes the following Unit 
Scheme : 

1. Short title and commencement, — -(1) This Scheme shall 
be called the Unit Scheme, 1976. (2) It shall come into force 
on January 1, 1976. 

2. Objective, — This Scheme primarily aim.s at securing for 
the unit holders capital appreciation on their investment in 
Units by investing the funds of the scheme in equity shares and 
convertible bonds/debentures of companies with good growth 
prospects. 

3. Definition.s, — ^In this Scheme, unless the context other- 
wise rcqpuircs — 

(a) the "Act" means the Unit Trust of India Act, 1963; 

(b) “acceptance date’’ with reference to an application 
made by an applicant to the Trust or its agent duly 
authorised in this behalf for sale or repurchase of 
units by the Trust means the day on which the Trust 
or its authorised agent, as the case may be, after 
being satisfied (bat such application is in order, 
accepts the same; 

(c) “body corporate" includes a society registered under 
the Societies Registration Act, 1860, such society 
being hereinafter referred to as “a society”; 

(d) "eligible trust" shall have the meaning a.ssigned to 
it under clause (aaa) of Regulation 2 of the Regd- 
lations. 

(e) “Regulations” means Unit Trust of India General 
Regulations, 1964 made under Section 43(1) of the 

I Act; 

(f) “number of units in issue” means the aggregate of 
the number of units sold and outstanding; 

(g) "recognised stock exchange” means a stock exchange 
which is, for the time being, recognised under the 
Securities Contracts (Regulation) Act, 1956 (42 of 
1956); 

(h) “scheme” means tb© Unit Scheme, 1976. 

(i) "Unit" means one undivided share of the face value 
of one hundred Rupees in the unit capital of this 
scheme; 

(j) all other expressions not defined herein but de- 
fined in the Act shall have the respective meanings 
assigned to them by the Act, 

4, Face Vti/ne of each imt.f.— The face value of each unit 
shall be one hundred rupees, 

5. AppUcatioTLS for unlt.s 

(1) Applications for units may be made by the follow- 
ing classes of persons : 

(i) An individual or individuals, none of whom i» 
a minor; 

(il) A company or other body corporate; ;

(iii) A company or other body corporate along with 
another company or body corporate or an indi- 
vidual or individuals, none of whom is a minor; 

(iv) A parent, step-parent or other lawful guardian 
on behalf of a minor; and 

(v) An eligible trust as defined in the Regulations in 
accordance with and to the extent provided in the 
Regulations, 

(2) An individual may make an application in his per- 
sonal capacity or in his capacity a.s an officer of a 
Government or of a Court. 

(3) An aplication shall not be made jointly on behalf of

to the provisioas of sub-clause (3), the total number 
of applicants in each case shall not exceed four. 

(3) The minimum number of units applied for shall be 
live and applications shall be for units in multiples 
of five. 

(6) (a) The payment for the units applied for by an 

applicant shall be made by him along with the 
application in cash, cheque, draft, postal order,

including the cost of realising the cheque, draft 
postal order, as the case may be, 

(b) If the payment is made by cheque or draft, the 
■acceptance date will, subject to such cheque or

.shall be refunded to him at his cost in such

(c) A nnit certificate will be sent by registered post

will not incur any liability for loss, damage,

cate. so sent. 

(7) No non-resident shall be eligible to apply for units 
under this scheme. 

fi. Application by and registration of bodies corporate and 
minors : 

( 1 ) A body corporate may be registered as a unit holder 
or one of joint unit holders. 

(2) An adult, being a parent, step-parent or other law- 
ful guardian of a minor may hold units and deal with 
them in accordance with and to the extent provided 
in sub^section (2A) of Section 21 of the Act. Such

(3) Applications by companies or other corporate bodies 
.shall be accompanied by the relevant documents 
.showing the applicant's competence to invest in units,

(5) Not more than four persons may be joint holders.

7, Holding by societies

pos.siblc, .send the applicant an acknowledgement therefor. As

cant one nnit certificate representing the units sold to turn,

10. Repurchase of units 

(1) The Trust shall, at any time, after the expiry of six 

months from the commencement of thi.s scheme on

ficate if such sale would result in his becoming

multiple of five. 

(ii) on sale of part of the units comprised in a unit 
certilicale, the Trust will issue to the seller a

him. 

(2) (1) The contract for repurchase shall be deemed to 

have been concluded on the acceptance date. 

(ii) Where an application for repurchase of units 
fi'om him has been accepted by the Trust, the 
Trust shall as soon thereafter as possible, send 
the applicant an acknowledgement therefor. 

(2) Payment for the units repurchased by the Tru.st shall 
be made as early as possible after the acceptance

in his application. No interest snail, on any account, 
be payable on the amount due to the applicant, and

draft sent by the Tiust shall be borne by the applicant. 

' 11. Restrictions on sale and repurchase of units. — Notwith- 
slanding any thing contained in any other provision of this 
scheme ; 

(a) the Trust shall not be under an obligation to sell or 
repurchase units 

(i) on such days as are not working days; and 

(ii) during the period (as notified by the Trust) 
when the register of unit holders is closed In 
connection with the annual closing of the 
books and accounts; and 

(b) the Trust shall not be under an obligation to repur- 
chase units during a period of six months from the 
commencement of this scheme. 

Explanation, — For the puiposes of this scheme, ■ the term 
“working day" shall mean a day which has not been either 
(i) notified under the Negotiable Instrupnents Act, 1881, to 
be a public holiday in the State of Maharashtra or such other 
States where the Trust has office.s, or (ii) notified by the Trust 
in the Gazette of India, as a day on which the head office of 
the Trust will be closed. 

12. Sale and Repurchase price ; 

(1) Hvery sale or repurchase of units by the Trust shall 
be as on the acceptance date at the respective prices 
prevailing on that day.

15. /'orni of unit certificate. — Unit certificates shall be in

mum of five units, 

17. Manner of preparation of unit certificate. — The unit 
certificates may be engraved or lithographed or printed as the

19. Exchange of unit certificates and procedure when certi- 
ficate is tnutiialed, defaced lost etc. 

(1) Subject to the provisions of this scheme, every unit 
holder shall be entitled to exchange any or all of 
his unit certificates for one or more unit certificates 
of such denominations in multiples of five units 
as he may require, representing the same aggregate 
number of units. While applying for such exchange, 
the unit holder shall surrender to the Trust the unit 
certificate or certificates to be exchanged and shall 
pay to the Trust all moneys (if any, payable there- 
under) in respect of the issue of the new unit certi- 
ficate or cerlifieales. 

(2) (a) In case anv unit certififatc is mulilaled or de- 
faced. the Trust in its discretion may issue to the 
person entitled a new unit certificate representing the 
same aggreguie mirnher of units as Ihc mutilated or 
defaced unit certificate represents. In case any unit 
certificate should be lost, stolen or destroyed, the 
Trust may, in its discretion issue to the person 
entitled a new unit certificate in lieu thereof. No 
such new unit certificate shall be issued unless the

applicant shall picvioiisiy have 

(i) furnished to the Tnist evidenee satisfactory to it 
of the mutilation, defacement, loss, theft or des- 
truction of the original unit ccitifieatc; 

(ii) paid all expenses in connection 'with the investig.a- 
lion of the facts; 

(iii) (in case of mutilation oi' defacement) produced 
and surrendered to the Trust the mutilated or 
defaced unit certificate; and 

(iv) furnished to the Trust such indemnity as it may 
require. 

fb) The Trust shall not incur any liability for issuing 
such ecitilicatc in good faith under the provisions of 
this clause. 

(3) Before issuing any certificate under the provisions of 
this clause, the Trust may require the applicant for 
the unit certificate to pay a fee of Rupee one per unit 
certificate issued by it together with a sum sufficient 
in the opinion of the Trust to cover stamp duty, 
if any, or other charges or taxes including postal 
registration charges that may be payable in connec-

(ii) Where .a unit certificate issued under sub-clause 
(7) of Clause 23 to a transferee is tendered for 
sub-division for the first time; and 

(iii) Corcsolidation of unit certificates. 

20. Rryistcr of unit holders. — The following provisions shall 
have elfect with regard to the registration of unit holders : — 

(1) A register of the unit holders shall be kept by the 
Trust and there shall be entered in the register : 

(a) the names and addresses of the unit holders; 

(b) the distinctive number of the unit certificate or 
certificates and the number of units held by 
every such person; and 

(c) I be dale on which such person became the 
holder of the units standing in his name. 

(2) No application for registration as a unit holder shall 
be entertained unless the application relates to a 
multiple of five units. 

Provided that where, on the death of a unit holder, 
any other other person becomes entitled to a number 
of units which is not a miilaplc of five, such person 
may be registered us a unit holder in respect of such 
number of units : 

Provided further that the Trust may, on applica- 
tion by such person in the appropriate form, sell to 
or repurchase from such person, such number of 
tmits as may be necessary to make the units held by 
him a multiple of five. 

Provided, however, that such person shall, within 
six months from the date of his being so registered, 
either purchase from or sell to the Trust units so as 
to make the number of units held by him a multiple 
of five. 

(3) If any unit certificate stands registered in the names 
of two or more persons, such persons shall be deem- 
ed to hold the unit certificate jointly and a discharge 
by the person first named in the register of unit 
holders shall, as regards receipt of amounts due in 
respect of such units, discharge the Tntst in respect 
of such amounts. 

(4) Where two or move individuals, none of them being 
a minor, apply, whether in pursuance of a transfer 
of units to them or olherwise. for issue of a unit 
certificate in their favour and, in such application or 
Otherwise, request in writing that any one of them 


should be permited to deal with the units represent- 
ed by the certificate, the Trust shall record in its 
books suitable entries to take note of such request; 
and when a unit certificate has been issued in such 
circumstances, then, notwithstanding anything to the 
contrary in sub-clause (3), any one or more of the 
holders shall be entitled to deal with the units re- 
presented by such certificate, and a discharge by any 
one or more of such persons shall, as regards receipt 
of amounts due in respect of snch units, discharge 
the Trust in respect of such amounts. 

(5) Any change of name or address on the part of any 
unit holder shall be notified to the Trust, which, on 
being satisfied of such change and on compliance 
with such formalities as it may require, shall alter 
the register accordingly. 

(6) Except when the register is closed in accordance with 
The provisions in that behalf hereinafter contained, 
the register .shall during business hours (subject to 
.such reasonable restrictions as the Trust may impose 
but so that not less than two hours on each 
business day shall be allowed for inspection) be 
open to inspection by ally unit holder, without 
charge. 

(7) The register will be closed at such .times and for 
such periods as the Trust may from time to time 
determine provided that it shall not be dosed for 
more than 30 days in any one year, the Trust 
shall give notice of such closure by advertisement in 
.such newspapers us the Board may direct. 

(z) No notice of any trust express, implied or construc - 
tive shall bo entered on the register in respect of anv 
unit. 

21. Receipt by unit \liolder to di.tchartte Trust, — The receipt 
of the unit holder for any moneys paid to him in respect of the 
units represented by the certificate shall be a good discharge 
to the Trust. 

22. Death or bankruptcy of a unit holder ; 

(1) In case of death of any one of the joint holders of 
a unit certificate, the survivor or survivors shall bo 
the only person (s) recognised by the Trust as having 
any title lo or interest in the units represented by the 
certificate. 

Provided that nothing herein contained shall affect 
any right which any other person may have as against 
such survivor or survivors in rc.spcct of the said 
units. 

(2) The executors or administrators of a deceased sole 
unit holder or a holder of succession certificate issued 
under Part X of the Indian Succession Act, 1925 
(39 of 1925) shall be the only persons who may bo 
recognised by the Trust as having any title to the 
unit. 

(3) Any person becoming entitled to a unit in conse- 
quence of the death or bankruptcy of any sole unit 
holder or of Ihc survivor or survivors of joint unit 
holders may subject as hejeinaftcr provided, upon 
producing such evidence as to his title as the Tnist 
shall consider sufficient, either be registered as the 
holder of such unit upon giving to the Trust notice 
in writing of his desire to the effect, or transfer such 
unit to some other person. All the limitations, res- 
trictions and provisions of this scheme relating to 
transfers shall be applicable to any such notice or 
transfer as if the death oi’ bankruptcy had not occur- 
red and snch notice or transfer were a transfer exe- 
cuted by the unit holder. 

(4) The Tiust may retain any moneys payable by it in 
respect of any unit of which any person is entitled 
to be registered as the unit holder or which any 
person under these provisions is entitled to transfer, 
until such person be registered as the holder of such 
unit or shall duly transfer the same. 

23. Transfer of units 

(1) Every unil holder shall be entitled to transfer the 
iinits or any of the units held by him by an inslrii- 
ment in writing in a form approved by the Chair- 
man of the Trust :

Provided that no transfer shall be registered if the

Piovided further that no transfer shall be made 
except to the persons in the classes mentioned iti 
clause 5- 

(2) Every instrument of transfer shall be signed by the 
transferor and the transferee and the transferor shall 
be deemed to remain the holder of the units trans- 
ferred until the name of the transferee is entered 
in the register in respect thereof. 

(3) Every instrument of transfer shall be duly stamped 
(if under the law it requires to be stamped) and left 
with the Trust for registration along with the relative 
unit certificate or certificates and such other evidence 
as the Trust may require in .support of the title of 
the transferor or his right to transfer the units. The 
Trust may dispense with the production of any unit 
certificates which shall have become lost, stolen or 
destroyed, upon compliance by the transferor with, 
the lik-c requirements to those arising in the case of 
an application by him for the replacement thereof. 

(4) When the units are issued in the official name, they 
shall be deemed to be transfciTed without any instru- 
ment of transfer from each holder of the office to 
the succeeding holder of the office on and from the 
date on which the latter takes charge of the office. 

(5) When the holder of the office transfers the units so 
held to a person not being his successor in office the 
transfer shall be made by an instrument of transfer 
signed by the holder of the office indicating the 
name of the office. 

(6) All instruments of transfer, which may be registered, 
shall he retained by the Trust. 

(7) Where uniw have been transferred, the Trust shall 
Issue to the transferee, whose name has been entered 
in the register, a fresh unit certificate in respect of 
the units transfened to him; where the transferor has 
transferred only a portion of the units covered by a 
certificate, the Trust shall issue to the (ransferor a 
fresh certificate for the units not transferred by him. 
Before issuing any certificate for the units transferred 
to the trnasfcree, the Trust may require the transferee 
to pay a fee of rupee one per certificate together with 
a sum sufficient in the opinion of the Trust to cover 
stamp duty, if any, or other charges or taxes that 
may be payable in connection with the issue of such 
certificate.

24. Applications and transfer forms signed by attorneys . — 
If any application or transfer form is signed by a person hold- 
ing a power of attorney empowering him to do so, the original 
power of attorney or a notarially certified copy of the same, 
should he submitted along with the application or the transfer 
form, as the case may be, unless the power of attorney has 
already been registered in the books of the Tnist. 

25. Nomination by Unit holders and Agents . — Unit holders 
and agents may exercise the right to make or cancel a nomi- 
nation to the extent provided in the Regulations. 

26. Payments to unitholders : 

(1) 1 he income to be distributed to the unit holders 
shall be paid, as soon as may be, after the closing of 
the annual accounts, as on the 30th June of each 
year, 

(2) It shall be lawful for a unit holder to receive and 
retain any income declared by the Trust in respect of 
units of which he is such holder, notwithstanding that 
the units have already been transferred by him for 
consideration unless the transferee who claims the 
income from the transferor has within fifteen days 
of the date on which the income became due, lodged 
the certificate and all other documents relating to the 
transfer which may, under the provisions of Clause 
23 or otherwise, he required by the Tnist, for being 
registered in his name. 


Explanation : The period specified in this sub clause shall 
he extended. — - 

(i) in case of death of the transferee, by the actual 
period taken by his legal representative to establish 
his claim to the income; 

fii) in Case of loss of the transfer deed by theft or any 
other cause beyond the control of the Iransferee, by 
the actual period taken for the replacement thereof; 

(iii) in case of delay in the lodging of any certificate and 
other documents relating to the transfer connected 
wilh transit through the post, by the actual period 
of the delay, 

(3) Nothing contained in sub-clause (2) shall affect the 
right of the Trust to pay to the unit holder any 
income, which has become due, in respect of units 
of which he is such a holder. 

(4) No interest shall be payable by the Trust on such 
income distributable among the unit holders. 

(5) The income distributable among the unit holders or 
any other money payable in respect of the units shall 
he paid by cheque or warrant drawn on the Trust’s 
bankers at the places where its offices are situated, 
or, at the option of the unit holder, by a Bank draft 
or money order, the charges for such bank draft or 
money order, being borne by the unit holder. 

27. Publication of accounts. — The Trust shall, a.s soon as 
may be after the 30th June of each year, cause to be published 
in such mannei- as the Board may specify accounts showing 
the working of the scheme during the period ending on the 
30(h Tune. 

28. Additions and amendments to Scheme. — The Board 
may from time to time add to or otherwise amend this Scheme 
and any amendment thereof will be notified in the official 
Ciazettc. 

29. Scheme to he binding on unit holders. — ^Thc terms of 
this scheme, including any amendments thereof from time to 
time, shall he binding on each unit holder and every other 
person claiming through him as if he had cxpres,sly agreed 
that they should be so binding. 

30. The provisions of the Scheme to he subject to the pro- 
visions of Plans. — The provisions of this scheme shall, in 
respect of any units acquired or held in accordance with Any 
plan or plans formulated bv the Board, pursuimt to danse (cc) 
of Sob-section (1) of Section 19 of the Act for acquisition of 
an interest m units, appiv and be read subject to the provi- 
sions of said plan or plans.

Corporation.”
